##  ०१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

सो᳓मो वै᳓ रा᳓जा यज्ञः᳓ प्रजा᳓पतिः ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

1. Verily, Prajāpati, the sacrifice, is King Soma;
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

सो᳘मो वै रा᳘जा यज्ञः᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिः॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

सो᳘मो वै रा᳘जा यज्ञः᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

**अथ ज्योतिष्टोमप्रायश्चित्तब्राह्मणम् ।** 

सोमो वै राजा यज्ञः प्रजापतिः ।
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः)</summary>

- सो᳓मः (*TS*-96/294, *RV*-229/728, *TB*-95/198, *TK*-2/4, *AV*-89/214, *SB-K*-223/436, *VS-M*-48/128, *VS-K*-36/107, *KS*-35/80, *MS*-133/396)
- वै᳓ (*TS*-2126, *TB*-1362, *AV*-194, *RV*-26, *TK*-135, *KS*-314, *MS*-3722)
- रा᳓जा (*TS*-30/48, *RV*-102/151, *TB*-46/63, *TK*-0/2, *AV*-60/78, *SB-K*-93/139, *VS-M*-19/27, *VS-K*-11/18, *KS*-14/23, *MS*-42/60)
- यज्ञः᳓ (*TS*-194/863, *RV*-48/448, *TB*-108/399, *TK*-12/21, *AV*-66/231, *SB-K*-599/1754, *VS-M*-25/223, *VS-K*-17/173, *KS*-47/171, *MS*-290/933)
- प्रजा᳓-पतिः (*TS*-172/437, *RV*-5, *TB*-165/248, *TK*-30/46, *AV*-50/76, *SB-K*-529/769, *VS-M*-23/44, *VS-K*-13/42, *KS*-34/48, *MS*-190/297)
</details>

<details><summary>हरिस्वामी</summary>

'सोमो वै राजा' । सोमप्रायश्चित्तब्राह्मणमेतत् । प्रायश्चित्तसम्बधार्थमेव पुनः सोमयागः प्रक्रियते । सोमो वै राजेति । सोमः अनतौधीषनां राजा । देवराजचंद्रमसो न न्यूनभूतः । तेनात्र यज्ञसामानाधिकरण्यान्मत्वर्थो लक्ष्यते । सोमसाधनको यज्ञः प्रजापतिरित्यधिदैवमित्यध्याहार्यम् । सोमसाधनको यज्ञः प्रजापतिः । तस्य एतास्तन्वः शरीराणि । या एता देवता वक्ष्यमाणाः परमेष्ठ्यादयः । प्रजापत्यात्मकस्य सोमस्येति विशेषाः । इत्यधिदेवम् ॥

याश्चैता आहुतीः । आहूयन्त इत्याहुतयः । परमेष्ठ्याद्याः ताः सोमद्रव्यात्मकस्य यज्ञस्य तन्वः । जुहोतीति क्रियात्मकस्य यज्ञस्य हवनक्रियास्तन्वः । एवं द्रव्यं देवता क्रिया च । पुनः यज्ञस्य द्रव्यदेवताक्रियाविशेषात्मिका एव प्रायश्चित्तीयास्तन्वः इत्युपन्यासः ॥ १ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This offering is to be made on the Āhavanīya, if it is during Dīkṣā, and the Upasada, on the Agnidhra, if it is during pressing of Soma plant; for, whatever joint of the sacrifice gets worn out, it breaks and whichever is the deity for that part of the sacrifice, he heals the sacrifice through that deity.
</details>

<details><summary>हरिस्वामी</summary>

'स यद्यज्ञस्य' । किं पुनर्नित्या एवैतास्तन्वः ? नेत्याह- यत्सोमयज्ञस्यांगम् आर्च्छेत् विनश्येत् । तदंगं तत्प्रति तस्मिन्नंगे यां देवतां परमेष्ठ्यादिकां मन्येत । "परमेष्ठ्यभिधीतः" (वा. सं. ८ । ५४) इत्यादेरागमात् । अवगच्छेच्च । तामनुपूर्वं सम्यक् ज्ञात्वा तस्यै देवतायै आज्याहुतिं जुहुयात् । यदि दीक्षोपवत्सु यज्ञस्य किंचिदार्च्छेत् तदा आहवनीये जुहुयात् । यदि तु प्रसुत्यायां तत आग्नीध्रीये । प्रायणीयादूर्ध्वं प्राक् सुत्यायाः क्व होतव्यं ? आहवनीये, कुतः ? "दीक्षोपसत्स्वाहवनीये" इत्यनुवादत एव प्राप्तत्वात् । एतावदेव ह्यनुविधीयते- "प्रसुत आग्नीध्रे" जुहोतीति । ततश्च सुत्यायामेव आग्नीध्रता प्राप्तेति । विस्रंसते वै एतद्यज्ञस्य पर्व अंगं यत् ह्वलति । स्वभावाद्विगुणं भवतीत्यर्थः । ततश्च 'या' एव पर्वपर्वणि अभिध्यानादौ देवता परमेष्ठ्यादिर्भवति । तस्या देवताया एतदेव कुर्वन् यज्ञं भिषज्यति संदधाति । इति संक्षेपवचनम् ॥ २ ॥
</details>

'स यद्येनम्' । विशेषवचनानि एतानि प्रसन्नानि । परमेष्ठी हि स यज्ञस्तदा भवति । अन्यया वा ऽऽहुत्या प्रतिबन्धकं पाप्मानं अपहन्ति यज्ञश्च तस्मिन्नपहते एनं यजमानं उपनमति ॥ ३-३७ ॥
</details>

</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

3. If, however, the sacrifice, resolved upon in his mind, were not to incline to him [^egg_553], let him perform an oblation with, 'To Parameshṭḥin hail!' for Parameshṭḥin (the most high) he (Soma [^egg_554]) then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.

[^egg_553]: 206:2 That is to say, if untoward circumstances were to arise threatening to prevent the intended Soma-sacrifice. The mental resolve (saṁkalpa), on the part of the Sacrificer, is the first act in the performance of a sacrifice.

[^egg_554]: 206:3 Or, it (the sacrifice), as Harisvāmin takes it.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

परमेष्ठि᳘ने स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्परमेष्ठी हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥ कांडस्यार्द्धम् २३०॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

परमेष्ठि᳘ने स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्परमेष्ठी हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

**"परमेष्ठिने स्वाहा"**- इति जुहुयात् परमेष्ठी हि स तर्हि भवति । अप पाप्मानं हते । उपैनं यज्ञो नमति ॥ ३ ॥
</details>

4. And if the sacrifice, bespoken by his speech [^egg_555] were not to incline to him, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Prajāpati hail!' for Prajāpati (the lord of creatures) he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.

##  ०५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓स्य रा᳓जानम᳓छेत्वा᳓ ।  
ना᳓ह᳓रन्त एयु᳓र᳓न्धसे स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद᳓न्धो हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/11_atha_yasya_rAjAnamaChetvA.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

5. And if any one's (people), having gone in quest of the King (Soma), do not come back bringing (Soma-plants), let him perform an oblation with, 'To the plant hail!' for the plant he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

If someone's (emissaries) having gone to fetch King Soma, return without securing him, let him offer an oblation (saying) "To the plants-Svāhā", for them he becomes the plant; he thus reples evil and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘स्य रा᳘जानम᳘च्छेत्त्वा᳘॥  
नाह᳘रन्त ऽएयुर᳘न्धसे स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद᳘न्धो हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘स्य रा᳘जानम᳘छेत्वा᳟᳟ ॥  
नाह᳘रन्त एयुर᳘न्धसे स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद᳘न्धो हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

अथ यस्य राजानमच्छेत्त्वा नाहरन्त एयुः । **"अन्धसे स्वाहा"**- इति जुहुयात् । अन्धो हि स तर्हि भवति । अप पाप्मानं हते । उपैनं यज्ञो नमति ॥ ५ ॥
</details>

##  ०६

<div class="js_include" includetitle="false" newlevelforh1="3" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarva-prastutiH/12/06/1/12_atha_yadi_sAtaH.md">
<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि सातः᳓ ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत सवित्रे᳓ स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयात्सविता᳓ हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

6. And if, when acquired, (his Soma). were to meet with any mishap, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Savitr̥ hail!' for Savitr̥ he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

After being procured, if something befalls on him (the Soma), let him make an offering (saying) "To Savitā-Svāhā", for he becomes Savitā then and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि सातः[[!!]]॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत सवित्रे स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्सविता हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘दि सातः᳟ ॥  
किं᳘चिदाप᳘द्येत सवित्रे स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्सविता हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

अथ यदि सातः किंचिदापद्येत । **"सवित्रे स्वाहा"**- इति जुहुयात् । सविता हि स तर्हि भवति । अप पाप्मानं हते । उपैनं यज्ञो नमति ॥ ६ ॥
</details>

##  ०७

<div class="js_include" includetitle="false" newlevelforh1="3" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarva-prastutiH/12/06/1/13_atha_yadi_dIxAsu.md">
<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि दीक्षा᳓सु ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत विश्व᳓कर्मणे स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद्विश्व᳓कर्मा हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

7. And if during the initiation (his Soma) were

to meet with any mishap, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Viśvakarman hail!' for Viśvakarman he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

If something happens to the Soma during the Dīkṣā let him make an offering (saying) “To Viśvakarmā-Svāhā”, for he becomes Viśvakarmā then and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि दीक्षा᳘सु॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत व्विश्व᳘कर्म्मणे स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्विश्व᳘कर्म्मा हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘दि दीक्षा᳘सु ॥  
किं᳘चिदाप᳘द्येत विश्व᳘कर्मणे स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्विश्व᳘कर्मा हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

##  ०८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि सोमक्र᳓यण्याम् ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत पूष्णे᳓ स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयात्पूषा᳓ हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/14_atha_yadi_somakrayaNyAm.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

8. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap in regard to the (cow) given in exchange for the Soma, let him perform an ablation with, 'To Pūshan hail!' for Pūshan he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

If something happens during the purchasing of Soma, let him make an offering (saying) "To Pūṣan-Svāhā", for then he becomes Pūṣan and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि सोमक्र᳘यण्याम्॥  
(ङ्कि᳘) कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत पूष्णे स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्पूषा हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘दि सोमक्र᳘यण्याम् ॥  
किं᳘चिदाप᳘द्येत पूष्णे स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्पूषा हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

अथ यदि सोमक्रयण्यां किंचिदापद्येत । **"पूष्णे स्वाहा"**- इति जुहुयात् । पूषा हि स तर्हि भवति । अप पाप्मानं हते । उपैनं यज्ञो नमति ॥ ८ ॥
</details>

##  ०९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि क्रया᳓योपो᳓त्थितः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येते᳓न्द्राय च मरु᳓द्भ्यश्च स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयादि᳓न्द्रश्च हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि मरु᳓तश्च भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/15_atha_yadi_krayAyopotthitaH.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

9. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap when forthcoming for the purchase, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Indra and the Maruts hail!' for Indra and the Maruts he (Soma) then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When the Soma has come forward for being bought and at that time some mishap befalls on him, let him make an offering (saying) “To Indra and the Maruts-Svāhā", for then he becomes Indra and the Maruts and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि क्रया᳘योपोत्थितः[[!!]]॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येते᳘न्द्राय च मरु᳘द्भ्यश्च स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयादि᳘न्द्रश्च हि स त᳘र्हि मरु᳘तश्च भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘दि क्रया᳘योपो᳘त्थितः ॥  
किं᳘चिदाप᳘द्येते᳘न्द्राय च मरु᳘द्भ्यश्च स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयादि᳘न्द्रश्च हि स त᳘र्हि मरु᳘तश्च भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

अथ यदि क्रयायोपोत्थितः किंचिदापद्येत । **"इन्द्राय च मरुद्भ्यश्च स्वाहा"**- इति । जुहुयात् । इन्द्रश्च हि स तर्हि मरुतश्च भवति । अप पाप्मानं हते । उपैनं यज्ञो नमति ॥ ९ ॥
</details>

##  ११

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/23 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि क्रीतः᳓ ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत मित्रा᳓य स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयान्मित्रो᳓ हि᳓ स᳓ [[तर्हि᳓|त᳓र्हि]] भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/17_atha_yadi_krItaH.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

11. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap after he has been bought, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Mitra hail!' for Mitra he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

If after being bought, something happens to him (Soma), let him offer an oblation (saying) "To Mitra-Svāhā", for he then becomes Mitra and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि क्रीतः[[!!]]॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत मित्रा᳘य स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयान्मित्रो हि स[[!!]] तर्हि᳘ भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘दि क्रीतः᳟ ॥  
किं᳘चिदाप᳘द्येत मित्रा᳘य स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयान्मित्रो हि स᳘ तर्हि᳘ भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

अथ यदि क्रीतः किंचिदापद्येत । **"मित्राय स्वाहा"**- इति जुहुयात् । मित्रो हि स तर्हि भवति । अप पाप्मानं हते । उपैनं यज्ञो नमति ॥ ११ ॥
</details>

##  १२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓द्यूरा᳓वा᳓सन्नः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत वि᳓ष्णवे शिपिविष्टा᳓य स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद्वि᳓ष्णुर्हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि शिपिविष्टो᳓ भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/18_atha_yadyUrAvAsannaH_kiMchidApadyeta.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

12. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap whilst seated on (the Sacrificer's) lap [^egg_556], let him perform an oblation with, 'To Vishṇu Śipivishṭa hail!' for Vishṇu Śipivishṭa he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.

[^egg_556]: 207:1 See III, 6, 3, 4. This particular ceremony is rather out of place here, as in its regular order it should come after paragraph 15.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

If something happens when he (Soma) is seated on the lap (of the Yajamāna), let him offer an oblation (saying) “To Viṣṇu the Śipiviṣṭa-Svāhā", for he then becomes Viṣṇu, the Śipiviṣṭa and then the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘द्यूरावा᳘सन्नः॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत व्वि᳘ष्णवे शिपिविष्टा᳘य स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्वि᳘ष्णुर्हि स त᳘र्हि शिपिविष्टो भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत वि᳓ष्णवे नरं᳓धिषाय स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद्वि᳓ष्णुर्हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि नरं᳓धिषो भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥

13. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap whilst being driven about, let him perform.

an oblation with, 'To Vishṇu Narandhisha hail! for Vishṇu Narandhisha he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

If something should befall on him (Soma) when he is being drawn about (in the cart), let him offer an oblation (saying) “To Viṣṇu, the Narandhiṣa-Svāhā, for he then becomes Viṣṇu, the Narandhiṣa and the sac- rifice inclines to him.
</details>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि पर्युह्य᳓मा᳓णः ।॥  
किं᳓चिद् आ᳓पद्येत वि᳓ष्णवे नरं᳓धिषा᳓य स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुया᳓द् वि᳓ष्णुर् हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि नरं᳓धिषो भ᳓वत्य् अ᳓प पा᳓प्मा᳓नँ हत᳓ उ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥॥

##  १४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓द्या᳓गतः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत सो᳓माय स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयात्सो᳓मो हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/20_atha_yadyAgataH_kiMchidApadyeta.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

14. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap when he has reached (the hall), let him perform an oblation with, 'To Soma hail!' for Soma he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When he (Soma) has arrived (at the hall) something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) "To Soma-Svāhā", for then he be- comes Soma and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ यद्या᳘गतः॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत सो᳘माय स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्सो᳘मो हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ यद्या᳘गतः ॥  
किं᳘चिदाप᳘द्येत सो᳘माय स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्सो᳘मो हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

अथ यद्यागतः किंचिदापद्येत । **"सोमाय स्वाहा"**- इति जुहुयात् । सोमो हि स तर्हि भवति । अप पाप्मानं हते । उपैनं यज्ञो नमति ॥ १४ ॥
</details>

15. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap when seated on the throne, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Varuṇa hail!' for Varuṇa he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When seated on the seat he (Soma) meets with some mishap, let him offer an oblation (saying) "To Varuṇa-Svāhā”, for he then becomes Varuṇa and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

16. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap whilst staying in the Āgnīdhra, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Agni hail!' for Agni he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.

##  १७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि हविर्धा᳓नगतः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येते᳓न्द्राय स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयादि᳓न्द्रो हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/23_atha_yadi_havirdhAnagataH.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

17. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap whilst staying in the Havirdhāna, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Indra hail!' for Indra he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When he (Soma) has reached the Havirdhāna, something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) "To Indra-Svāhā", for then he becomes Indra and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि हविर्द्धा᳘नगतः॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येते᳘न्द्राय स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयादि᳘न्द्रो हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

18. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap whilst being taken down (from the ear), let him perform an oblation with, 'To Atharvan hail!' for Atharvan he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

If something happens to him (Soma) when being taken down from the cart, let him offer an oblation (saying) "To Atharvan-Svāhā", for he then becomes Atharva and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘द्युपावह्रिय᳘माणः॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येता᳘थर्व्वणे स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद᳘थर्व्वा हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

##  १९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓द्यंशु᳓षु न्यु᳙प्तः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत वि᳓श्वेभ्यो देवे᳓भ्यः स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद्वि᳓श्वे हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि देवा᳓ भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/25_atha_yadyaMshuShu_nyuptaH.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

19. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap when thrown down (on the pressing-board) in (the shape of) the Soma-stalks, let him perform an oblation with, 'To the All-gods hail!' for the All-gods he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When he (Soma) is thrown down as Soma-stalks (on the pressing board), something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) "To Viśvedevas-Svāhā", for then he becomes the Viśvedevas and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘द्यᳫँ᳭शु᳘षु᳘ न्युप्तः॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत व्वि᳘श्वेभ्यो देवे᳘भ्यः स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्वि᳘श्वे हि स त᳘र्हि देवा[[!!]] भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘द्यᳫंशु᳘षुॗ न्युप्तः ॥  
किं᳘चिदाप᳘द्येत वि᳘श्वेभ्यो देवे᳘भ्यः स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्वि᳘श्वे हि स त᳘र्हि देवा᳘ भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥
</details>

##  २०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓द्याप्याय्य᳓मानः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत वि᳓ष्णवआप्रीतपा᳓य स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद्वि᳓ष्णुर्हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्ह्याप्रीतपा᳓ भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/26_atha_yadyApyAyyamAnaH_kiMchidApadyeta.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

20. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap whilst being invigorated (moistened), let him perform an oblation with, 'To Vishṇu Āprītapā hail!' for Vishṇu Āprītapā (the protector of the appeased) he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

While he (Soma) is invigorated (drenched with water for pressing), something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) “To Viṣṇu, the Aprītapa-Svāhā", for then he becomes Viṣṇu, the Aprītapa and the sacri- fice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘द्याप्याय्य᳘मानः॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत व्वि᳘ष्णव ऽआप्प्री᳘तपा᳘य स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्वि᳘ष्णुर्हि स त᳘र्ह्याप्प्रीतपा भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘द्याप्याय्य᳘मानः ॥  
किं᳘चिदाप᳘द्येत वि᳘ष्णवऽआप्रीतपा᳘य स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्वि᳘ष्णुर्हि स त᳘र्ह्याप्रीतपा भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥

##  २१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓द्यभिषूय᳓माणः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत यमा᳓य स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद्यमो᳓ हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

21. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap whilst being pressed, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Yama hail!' for Yama he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

While being pressed something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) "To Yama-Svāhā", for then he becomes Yama and the sac- rifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘द्यभिषूय᳘माणः॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत यमा᳘य स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्यमो हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

##  २२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि सम्भ्रिय᳓माणः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत वि᳓ष्णवे स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद्वि᳓ष्णुर्हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/28_atha_yadi_sambhriyamANaH.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

22. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap whilst being gathered together [^egg_557], let him perform an oblation with, 'To Vishṇu hail!' for Vishṇu he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.

[^egg_557]: 209:1 See III, 9, 4, 19, 'Thrice he presses, and thrice he gathers (the beaten plants) together . . .'
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

While being gathered together (in between the pressings) something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) "To Viṣṇu-Svāhā", for he then becomes Viṣṇu and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि सम्भ्रिय᳘माणः॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत व्वि᳘ष्णवे स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्वि᳘ष्णुर्हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

##  २३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि पूय᳓मानः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत वाय᳓वे स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद्वायु᳓र्हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/29_atha_yadi_pUyamAnaH.md">

23. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap whilst being purified (strained), let him perform an oblation with, 'To Vāyu hail!' for Vāyu he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

While being purified(strained), (strained) something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) “To Vāyu-Svāhā", for he then becomes Vāyu and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत व्वाय᳘वे स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्वायुर्हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

##  २५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि क्षीरश्रीः᳓ ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत शुक्रा᳓य स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाछुक्रो᳓ हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/31_atha_yadi_xIrashrIH.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

25. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap when mixed with milk, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Śukra hail!' for Śukra he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

While being mixed, with milk, if something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) "To Śukra-Svāhā", for he then becomes Śukra and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि क्षीरश्रीः[[!!]]॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत शुक्रा᳘य स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाच्छुक्रो हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

##  २६

<div class="js_include" includetitle="false" newlevelforh1="3" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarva-prastutiH/12/06/1/32_atha_yadi_saktushrIH.md">
<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि सक्तुश्रीः᳓ ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत मन्थि᳓ने स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयान्मन्थी᳓ हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

26. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any

mishap when mixed with barley-meal, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Manthin hail!' for Manthin (Soma mixed with meal) he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When being mixed with barley flour, if something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) - "To Manthin-Svāhā", for he then be- come Manthin and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि सक्तुश्रीः[[!!]]॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत मन्थि᳘ने स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयान्मन्थी हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

##  २७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि चमसे᳓षू᳓न्नीतः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत वि᳓श्वेभ्यो देवे᳓भ्यः स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद्वि᳓श्वे हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि देवा᳓ भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

27. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap when drawn into the cups, let him perform an oblation with, 'To the All-gods hail!' for the All-gods he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When collected in the Grahas, if something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) - "To the Viśvedevas-Svāhā", for he then becomes Viśvedevas and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि चमसेषू᳘न्नीतः॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत व्वि᳘श्वेभ्यो देवे᳘भ्यः स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्वि᳘श्वे हि स त᳘र्हि देवा[[!!]] भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

##  २८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि हो᳓मायो᳓द्यतः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येता᳓सवे स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद᳓सुर्हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

28. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap when ready for the libation, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Asu hail!' for Asu (the breath of life) he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When ready for offering, if something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) - "To Asu-Svāhā", for he then becomes Asu and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

</details>

##  ३०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓द्यभ्या᳓वृत्तः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत वा᳓ताय स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद्वा᳓तो हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

30. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap when he has returned [^egg_558], let him perform an oblation with, 'To Vāta hail!' for Vāta (the wind) he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.

[^egg_558]: 210:1 Viz. to the Havirdhāna where the cups from which libations have been made are deposited on the mound (khara); cf. III, 1, 2, 24.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When he has returned (to the Havirdhāna), if something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) - "To Vāta-Svāhā", for he then becomes Vāta and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

##  ३१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि प्र᳓तिख्यातः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत नृच᳓क्षसे स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयान्नृच᳓क्षा हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

31. And if, after being looked at, (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Nr̥cakshas hail!' for Nr̥cakshas (man-viewing) he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

After being looked at, if something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) "To Nṛcakṣas-Svāhā", for he then becomes Nṛcakṣas and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

##  ३२

<div class="js_include" includetitle="false" newlevelforh1="3" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarva-prastutiH/12/06/1/38_atha_yadi_bhaxyamANaH.md">
<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि भक्ष्य᳓माणः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत भक्षा᳓य स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयाद्भक्षो᳓ हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

32. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap whilst being consumed, let him perform an oblation with, 'To Bhaksha hail!' for Bhaksha

 (drink) he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

While being drunk, if something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) "To Bhakṣa-Svāhā", for he then becomes Bhakṣa (drink) and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत भक्षा᳘य स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयाद्भक्षो हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

##  ३३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि नाराशंसे᳓षु सन्नः᳓ ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत पितृ᳓भ्यो नाराशंसे᳓भ्यः स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयात्पित᳓रो हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि नाराशंसा᳓ भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/39_atha_yadi_nArAshaMseShu.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

33. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap when contained in the Nārāśaṁsa (cups [^egg_559]), let him perform an oblation with, 'To the Nārāśaṁsa Fathers hail!' for the Nārāśaṁsa (man-praising) Fathers he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.

[^egg_559]: 211:1 See part ii, p. 154, note 1.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When kept in the Nārāśaṁsa cups, if something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) - "To the Nārāśaṁsa Pitṛs-Svāhā", for he then becomes the Nārāśaṁsa Pitṛ and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि नाराशᳫँ᳭से᳘षु सन्नः[[!!]]॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत पितृ᳘भ्यो नाराशᳫँ᳭से᳘भ्यः स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्पित᳘रो हि स त᳘र्हि नाराशᳫँ᳭सा भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘दि नाराशᳫंसे᳘षु सन्नः᳟ ॥  
किं᳘चिदाप᳘द्येत पितृ᳘भ्यो नाराशᳫंसे᳘भ्यः स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्पित᳘रो हि स त᳘र्हि नाराशᳫंसा भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

अथ यदि नाराशंसेषु सन्नः किंचिदापद्येत । **पितृभ्यो नाराशंसेभ्यः स्वाहा"**- इति जुहुयात् । पितरो हि स तर्हि नाराशंसा भवति । अप पाप्मानं हते । उपैनं यज्ञो नमति ॥ ३३ ॥
</details>

अ᳓थ य᳓द्यभ्यवह्रिय᳓माणः ।  
कि᳓ञ्चिदाप᳓द्येत समुद्रा᳓य स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयात्समुद्रो᳓ हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हत उ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ।  
॥

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

35. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap whilst being taken down (to the water), let him perform an oblation with, 'To the Sea hail!' for a sea he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

While being taken down (to the water) if something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) - “To Samudra-Svāhā.” For he then be- comes Samudra and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

##  ३६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः - ब्राह्मणम्</summary>

अ᳓थ य᳓दि प्र᳓प्लुतः ।  
किं᳓चिदाप᳓द्येत सलिला᳓य स्वा᳓हे᳓ति जुहुयात्सलिलो᳓ हि᳓ स᳓ त᳓र्हि भ᳓वत्य᳓प पाप्मा᳓नं हतउ᳓पैनं यज्ञो᳓ नमति ॥
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/12/06/1/42_atha_yadi_praplutaH.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

36. And if (his Soma) were to meet with any mishap when immersed, let him perform an oblation with, 'To the Flood hail!' for a flood he then is: he repels evil, and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When immersed (in the water) if something happens to him, let him offer an oblation (saying) - “To Salila-Svāhā”. For he then becomes salila(water) and the sacrifice inclines to him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

(त्य᳘) अ᳘थ य᳘दि प्र᳘प्लुतः॥  
कि᳘ञ्चिदाप᳘द्येत सलिला᳘य स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्सलिलो हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫँ᳭ हत ऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

अ᳘थ य᳘दि प्र᳘प्लुतः ॥  
किं᳘चिदाप᳘द्येत सलिला᳘य स्वाहे᳘ति जुहुयात्सलिलो हि स त᳘र्हि भ᳘वत्य᳘प पाप्मा᳘नᳫं हतऽउ᳘पैनं यज्ञो᳘ नमति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

अथ यदि प्रप्लुतः किंचिदापद्येत । **"सलिलाय स्वाहा"**- इति जुहुयात् । सलिलो हि स तर्हि भवति । अप पाप्मानं हते । उपैनं यज्ञो नमति ॥ ३६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>हरिस्वामी</summary>

बन्धुता ब्राह्मणसमूहः । तां इन्द्रः अभिध्यातवान् । यदि मह्यं विराजं ब्रवीषि, तर्हि ममाहंजातीयं रूपं अहं त्वा दर्शयेयेति । तथा करोमि यथा मदीयं रूपं अवश्यं त्वां दर्शयेय तथा सर्वस्य यज्ञस्य प्रायश्चित्तिं च ब्रूयात् । त्वयोक्तं रूपं दर्शयिष्यामीति । किन्न्वेव त्वदीयरूपेण दृष्टेनेत्येतदाह- किं सः स्यात् । कीदृशः स स्यात् । यं मां स्वमध्यात्मं रूपं दर्शयेथाः । तत्राह- जीवस्वर्ग इति । जीवतां स्वर्गो जीवस्वर्गः तस्मिन् जीवस्वर्गे एव स प्रेयात् प्रगच्छेत् । तस्माल्लोकात् येन यत्र स्वर्गे शक्यं गन्तुम् । तत्र मद्दशां गच्छेदित्यर्थः । इयं वै पृथिवी दिग्भेदेन दशाक्षरात्मकं छन्दः विराजो ऽधिदैवतम् । इत्येतां विराजमुवाच । अग्निहोत्रमारभ्य दीर्घसत्रं ह वा एत उपयन्ति इति या एताः सौमिक्यः प्रायश्चित्तयः प्रकृतिप्रविष्टाः सन्ति । ताः चोदकपरंपरया संवत्सरसत्रेष्वतिदिष्टाः तत्र च महदुक्थ्यम्- उपांत्ये दिवसे । तेन एतदुक्तं भवति । आ संवत्सरसत्रेभ्य इति [^१_८३] ॥ ३८-४१ ॥

इति श्रीमदाचार्यहरिस्वामिनः कृतौ माध्यन्दिनीयशतपथब्राह्मणभाष्ये द्वादशकांडे षष्ठे ऽध्याये प्रथमं ब्राह्मणम् ॥ १ ॥

नागस्वामिसुतो ऽवन्त्यां पाराशर्यो वसन् हरिः । 
श्रुत्यर्थं दर्शयामास शक्तितः पौष्करीयकः ॥

श्रीमतो ऽवन्तिनाथस्य विक्रमार्कस्य भूपतेः । 
धर्माध्यक्षो हरिस्वामी व्याख्यच्छातपथीं श्रुतिम् ॥

भूभर्त्रा विक्रमार्केण क्लृप्तां कनकवेदिकाम् । 
दानायाध्यास्य कृतवान् श्रुत्यर्थविवृतिं हरिः ॥

इति श्रीसर्वविद्यानिधानकवीन्द्राचार्यसरस्वतीनां श्रीहरिस्वामिनां कृतौ माध्यन्दिनीयशतपथब्राह्मणभाष्ये द्वादशकाण्डे षष्ठो ऽध्यायः समाप्तः ॥ १२-६ ॥

**उत्तरक्रतुनिरूपकं प्रकरणं समाप्तम् ॥**

[^१_८३]: महर्षिः कात्यायनः श्रुत्यनुयायिसूत्रजातमरीचत् । परमेष्ठ्यादींश्चतुस्त्रिँशतं जुहोति । घर्मधुग्घ्वाले चादोहे च । उदीच्या दोहस्थाने ऽन्यस्याः । शालाया वा पुरस्तात्प्राच्याः । पुच्छकांडाद्दक्षिणे ऽस्थनि हुत्वा दोहयेत् । पृषदाज्यस्कन्दने चैके । सोमेज्योपपाते चैकैकां यथाकालँ हुत्वा यज्ञस्य दोह इति वाचयति । आग्नीध्रीये सुत्यासु । का. श्रौ. सू. २५ । १३७-१४४ ।
</details>

<div class="js_include" includetitle="false" newlevelforh1="3" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarva-prastutiH/12/06/1/49_brahmANaM_tvAmantrya.md">
<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ब्रह्मा᳓णं त्वा᳙म᳓न्त्र्य ब्रह्मणा᳓तिसृष्टस्ता᳓सां वा᳓एता᳓सां व्या᳓हृतीनां बन्धु᳓ता ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

38. The Brahman (superintending priest) himself should perform them, and no other than the Brahman; for the Brahman sits on the right (south)

side of the sacrifice, and protects the sacrifice on the right side. If, however, the Brahman should not know (these formulas and oblations), any one who knows them may perform them; but (let him do so) after applying for leave to the Brahman, and with his permission.
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

For the Brahmā sits to the south of the Yajamāna and protects the sacrifice from the right side. If Brahmā is not available, whoever is there who knows (these mantras) may offer. But he should take the permission of Brahmā and after being authorised by Brahman.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

ब्रह्मा᳘ण᳘न्त्वाम᳘न्त्र्य ब्रह्मणा᳘ ऽतिसृष्टस्ता᳘साम्वा᳘ ऽएता᳘साम्व्या᳘हृतीनाम्बन्धु᳘ता
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

ब्रह्मा᳘णंॗ त्वाम᳘न्त्र्य ब्रह्मणा᳘तिसृष्टस्ता᳘सां वा᳘ऽएता᳘सां व्या᳘हृतीनां बन्धु᳘ता
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

ब्रह्माणं त्वामंत्र्य । ब्रह्मणा ऽतिसृष्टः । तासां वा एतासां व्याहृतीनां बन्धुता ।
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

व᳓सिष्ठो ह विरा᳓जं विदां᳓ चकार तां᳓ हे᳓न्द्रोऽभि᳓दध्यौ ॥

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

Now as to the meaning of these (formulas). Vasishṭḥa knew the Virāj [^egg_561]: Indra coveted it.

[^egg_561]: 212:1 That is, the 'far-shining;' or 'far-ruling' (metre).
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Now about the meaning of these mantras. Vasiṣṭha knew the Virāḍ and Indra coveted it.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

व्व᳘सिष्ठो ह व्विरा᳘जम्विदा᳘ञ्चकार ताᳫँ᳭ हे᳘न्द्रो ऽभि᳘दध्यौ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

व᳘सिष्ठो ह विरा᳘जं विदां᳘ चकार ताᳫं हे᳘न्द्रोऽभि᳘दध्यौ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

वसिष्ठो ह विराजं विदांचकार । तां हेन्द्रो ऽभिदध्यौ ॥ ३८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

प्रा᳓यश्चित्तिमुवाचाग्निहोत्रा᳓द᳓ग्रआ᳓ महत᳓ उक्था᳓त्ता᳓ ह स्मैताः᳓ पुरा᳓ व्या᳓हृतीर्व᳓सिष्ठा एव᳓ विदुः ।
</details>

And, indeed, he who thus knows these utterances is worthy to become

</details>
